# Melodiespark Web3 Wallet EXE — 1,000-Requirement A–Z Build Specification

> Purpose: build a legitimate Windows wallet and revenue-operations application. This specification does not create money, scrape funds, custody private keys, or authorize silent transfers. Revenue becomes real only after a real customer payment, fulfillment evidence, provider confirmation, reconciliation and an audit record.

## Non-negotiable operating invariants

- Never store, request, log or transmit a seed phrase or raw private key.
- Use MetaMask, WalletConnect or a hardware wallet for external signing.
- Keep commercial revenue, blockchain assets, rewards/points and testnet assets in separate ledgers.
- Run background discovery and reconciliation read-only; require explicit user approval for transfers, refunds, swaps, payouts and broadcasts.
- Treat provider webhooks as untrusted until signature verification, replay protection and reconciliation succeed.
- Label simulated, testnet, pending, disputed, restricted and non-redeemable balances clearly; exclude them from revenue totals.
- Preserve an append-only evidence trail with hashes, timestamps, source identifiers and verification results.
- Default to deny when configuration, ownership, destination, network, amount, fee, tax or evidence is uncertain.

## Acceptance formula

`REAL CUSTOMER + REAL PRODUCT + REAL PAYMENT + DELIVERY/FULFILLMENT + VERIFIED PROVIDER EVIDENCE + RECONCILIATION + AUDIT RECORD = VERIFIED REAL REVENUE`

## 1,000 implementation requirements

### A. Authority & Governance

1. **A-0001 — Define:** Create an explicit definition and machine-readable schema for the governance policy, including owner, approver, auditor and operator roles. Status must be one of DISABLED, CONFIGURED, CONNECTED, OBSERVED, VERIFIED, BLOCKED or FAILED; never infer VERIFIED or REAL_REVENUE. Acceptance evidence: automated test result, configuration fingerprint, timestamped audit event and authoritative-source reference.
2. **A-0002 — Validate:** Reject malformed, incomplete, placeholder or contradictory data in the governance policy, including owner, approver, auditor and operator roles. Status must be one of DISABLED, CONFIGURED, CONNECTED, OBSERVED, VERIFIED, BLOCKED or FAILED; never infer VERIFIED or REAL_REVENUE. Acceptance evidence: automated test result, configuration fingerprint, timestamped audit event and authoritative-source reference.
3. **A-0003 — Authorize:** Enforce least-privilege authorization before any operation involving the governance policy, including owner, approver, auditor and operator roles. Status must be one of DISABLED, CONFIGURED, CONNECTED, OBSERVED, VERIFIED, BLOCKED or FAILED; never infer VERIFIED or REAL_REVENUE. Acceptance evidence: automated test result, configuration fingerprint, timestamped audit event and authoritative-source reference.
4. **A-0004 — Configure:** Provide environment-specific configuration with safe defaults for the governance policy, including owner, approver, auditor and operator roles. Status must be one of DISABLED, CONFIGURED, CONNECTED, OBSERVED, VERIFIED, BLOCKED or FAILED; never infer VERIFIED or REAL_REVENUE. Acceptance evidence: automated test result, configuration fingerprint, timestamped audit event and authoritative-source reference.
5. **A-0005 — Persist:** Store durable state transactionally and retain provenance for the governance policy, including owner, approver, auditor and operator roles. Status must be one of DISABLED, CONFIGURED, CONNECTED, OBSERVED, VERIFIED, BLOCKED or FAILED; never infer VERIFIED or REAL_REVENUE. Acceptance evidence: automated test result, configuration fingerprint, timestamped audit event and authoritative-source reference.
6. **A-0006 — Encrypt:** Protect sensitive data in transit and at rest for the governance policy, including owner, approver, auditor and operator roles. Status must be one of DISABLED, CONFIGURED, CONNECTED, OBSERVED, VERIFIED, BLOCKED or FAILED; never infer VERIFIED or REAL_REVENUE. Acceptance evidence: automated test result, configuration fingerprint, timestamped audit event and authoritative-source reference.
7. **A-0007 — Observe:** Emit structured logs, metrics and trace identifiers for the governance policy, including owner, approver, auditor and operator roles. Status must be one of DISABLED, CONFIGURED, CONNECTED, OBSERVED, VERIFIED, BLOCKED or FAILED; never infer VERIFIED or REAL_REVENUE. Acceptance evidence: automated test result, configuration fingerprint, timestamped audit event and authoritative-source reference.
8. **A-0008 — Audit:** Append a tamper-evident audit event for every state change to the governance policy, including owner, approver, auditor and operator roles. Status must be one of DISABLED, CONFIGURED, CONNECTED, OBSERVED, VERIFIED, BLOCKED or FAILED; never infer VERIFIED or REAL_REVENUE. 9. **A-0009 — Test:** Add deterministic unit and integration tests covering the governance policy, including owner, approver, auditor and operator roles. ## Release gates

- Gate 1 — Architecture and threat model approved.
- Gate 2 — Database migrations and rollback verified.
- Gate 3 — Payment provider test environment verified without claiming revenue.
- Gate 4 — External wallet connection and human signing verified.
- Gate 5 — Commercial revenue evidence and reconciliation verified with a real authorized transaction.
- Gate 6 — Windows EXE signed, scanned, installed, upgraded and uninstalled successfully.
- Gate 7 — Backup restoration, incident response and kill switch tested.
- Gate 8 — Production launch explicitly approved by the owner.

Generated requirement count: **1000**.
